Licenças das dependências Rust do AnkiOS

Inventário gerado a partir dos Cargo.lock de AnkiOS/AnkiBackendBridge e AnkiOS/FSRSBridge. Inclui dependências específicas de outras plataformas e ferramentas de compilação registradas nos grafos Rust; nem todos os pacotes listados entram no binário iOS. As versões, expressões de licença, origem e manifesto de cada pacote estão em Tools/Legal/rust-crate-licenses.csv.

Pacotes sem arquivo de licença publicado dentro do pacote estão identificados pela expressão SPDX e pelo repositório no CSV.

Textos de licença publicados pelos pacotes:

Licenses for support code
-------------------------

in BoringSSL (i.e. libcrypto and libssl) when compiled, however, so
distributing code linked against BoringSSL does not trigger this license:

contributors may be used to endorse or promote products derived from

1. Definitions

1.1. "Contributor"

    means Covered Software of a particular Contributor.

    means Source Code Form to which the initial Contributor has attached
    the notice in Exhibit A, the Executable Form of such Source Code

    including portions thereof.

        terms of a Secondary License.

    means any form of the work other than Source Code Form.

    means a work that combines Covered Software with other material, in
    a separate file or files, that is not Covered Software.

1.8. "License"
    means this document.

1.9. "Licensable"
    means having the right to grant, to the maximum extent possible,

1.10. "Modifications"

    (a) any file in Source Code Form that results from an addition to,

        Software; or

    (b) any new file in Source Code Form that contains any Covered

    means any patent claim(s), including without limitation, method,

    means an individual or a legal entity exercising rights under this

5.2. "Data Provider" means any person who is the source of Data

when it starts in an interactive mode:

    Gnomovision comes with ABSOLUTELY NO WARRANTY; for details type `show w'.
    This is free software, and you are welcome to redistribute it
    under certain conditions; type `show c' for details.

parts of the General Public License.  Of course, the commands you use may
be called something other than `show w' and `show c'; they could even be
mouse-clicks or menu items--whatever suits your program.

necessary.  Here is a sample; alter the names:
